'use strict';
// Authored GEOMETRY INPUT only. Native loaders, queries and triangle/cache
// builders produce all sector ownership, contacts, normals, planes and motion.
const assert=require('node:assert/strict');
function chunk(id,data){const b=Buffer.alloc(8+data.length);b.writeUInt32LE(id>>>0);b.writeUInt32LE(data.length,4);data.copy(b,8);return b;}
function make(){
 const directory=Buffer.alloc(4);directory.writeUInt32LE(10);
 const sector=Buffer.alloc(36+4*8);sector.writeInt16LE(101,8);sector.writeInt16LE(4,10);[-128,-128,128,128].forEach((v,i)=>sector.writeFloatLE(v,12+i*4));
 const points=[[-128,-128],[128,-128],[128,128],[-128,128]];points.forEach((p,i)=>p.forEach((v,j)=>sector.writeFloatLE(v,36+i*8+j*4)));
 const metadata=chunk(0x80034150,Buffer.concat([chunk(0x34151,directory),chunk(0x34152,sector)]));

 // payload parent+16, satisfying original17004's aligned34131 contract.
 const header=Buffer.alloc(48);header.writeUInt16LE(111,0);header[2]=0;header[3]=0;header.writeUInt16LE(8,4);header.writeUInt16LE(8,6);header.writeFloatLE(-128,8);header.writeFloatLE(-128,12);[-128,-128,-1].forEach((v,i)=>header.writeFloatLE(v,16+i*4));[128,128,1].forEach((v,i)=>header.writeFloatLE(v,32+i*4));
 const packed=Buffer.alloc(32);packed[0]=0;packed[1]=0x90;packed[2]=0;packed[3]=0x10;packed.writeUInt16LE(0,4);packed.writeInt16LE(-1,6);points.forEach((p,i)=>{packed.writeInt16LE(p[0]*8,8+i*2);packed.writeInt16LE(p[1]*8,16+i*2);packed.writeInt16LE(0,24+i*2);});
 const indices=Buffer.alloc(4),membership=Buffer.alloc(4),cells=Buffer.alloc(64*4);membership.writeUInt16LE(0,0);membership.writeUInt16LE(0xffff,2);for(let i=0;i<64;i++){cells.writeUInt16LE(0,i*4);cells.writeUInt16LE(1,i*4+2);}
 const geometry=chunk(0x80034130,Buffer.concat([chunk(0x34131,header),chunk(0x34134,packed),chunk(0x34135,indices),chunk(0x34136,membership),chunk(0x34137,cells)]));
 assert.equal(geometry.readUInt32LE(8),0x34131);assert.equal(geometry.readUInt16LE(16),111);assert.equal(packed.readInt16LE(6),-1);

'use strict';
// Read-only evidence and assertions. No native API calls and no guest-memory writes.
const fs=require('node:fs'),path=require('node:path'),assert=require('node:assert/strict');
const GLOBAL=8978732,LABEL=8020800,STRIDE=128;
function create({directory}){
  let before=null,world=null,result=null;
  const proofName='native-ordered-history-pool-proof.json';
  assert(!fs.existsSync(path.join(directory,proofName)));
  const readable=(f,p,n)=>Number.isSafeInteger(p)&&p>=0x10000&&p+n<=f.memory.buffer.byteLength;
  const header=(f,p)=>({address:p,rawHex:f.bytes(p,64).toString('hex'),label:f.get(p+12),freeHead:f.get(p+16),flags:f.get(p+20),allocated:f.get(p+24),highWater:f.get(p+28),allocationFlags:f.get(p+32),blockCapacity:f.get(p+36),stride:f.get(p+40),totalCapacity:f.get(p+44)});

  function beforeWorld({q}){
    assert(!before);const{f}=q;
    before={pool:f.get(GLOBAL),client:f.get(8808948),host:f.get(8808980),online:f.view.getUint8(9031529),nativeWorld:f.get(8978560)};
    assert.equal(before.pool,0,'Fresh native extrapolated-state pool must be absent before its whole World producer');
    assert(before.client>0,'Existing whole17624 must produce the actual pending-client predicate before World');
    save();return before;
  }
  function afterWorld({q,Shared,receiver}){
    assert(before);assert(!world);const{f,A}=q,pool=f.get(GLOBAL);
    assert(readable(f,pool,64),'Whole1690 must publish an actual nonnull extrapolated-state pool');
    const actual=header(f,pool),heap=Shared.bind(f,{cpu:A.cpu}).heap;
    const containing=[...heap.owned.values()].filter(item=>item.live&&pool>=item.address&&pool+64<=item.address+item.bytes);
    assert.equal(containing.length,1,'Pool header must lie within exactly one actual live platform heap allocation');
    const allocation=containing[0],nativeAllocatorInteriorOffset=pool-allocation.address;
    assert.equal(actual.label,LABEL);assert.equal(actual.stride,STRIDE);assert.equal(actual.flags,11);
    assert(actual.blockCapacity>0);assert(actual.totalCapacity>=actual.blockCapacity);
    assert(pool+48+STRIDE*actual.blockCapacity<=allocation.address+allocation.bytes,'The entire native initial pool block must fit within its containing allocation');
    assert.equal(actual.allocated,0,'Capture the native unused history pool immediately after whole1690');
    assert.equal(actual.freeHead,pool+48,'Native3178 must own the initial free list');
    world={wholeProducer:1690,wrapper:21991,receiver,pool,header:actual,allocation:{...allocation},nativeAllocatorInteriorOffset,nativeAllocatorPrefixHex:f.bytes(allocation.address,Math.min(nativeAllocatorInteriorOffset,128)).toString('hex'),nativePublicationObservedAfterWholeReturn:true,poolMetadataOrLinksAuthored:false};
    save();return world;
  }
  function finish({q,enrollment,legacy}){
    assert(world);assert(!result);assert(legacy?.complete);assert(enrollment?.completed);
    const{f}=q,pool=f.get(GLOBAL);assert.equal(pool,world.pool,'Retain the same native pool throughout enrollment');
    const firstNode=pool+48,nodeAreaEnd=firstNode+STRIDE*world.header.blockCapacity;
    const initialNode=p=>readable(f,p,STRIDE)&&p>=firstNode&&p+STRIDE<=nodeAreaEnd&&(p-firstNode)%STRIDE===0;
    assert.equal(f.get(pool+8),0,'This one-node unit must not require a native growth block');
    assert.equal(f.get(pool+44),world.header.totalCapacity,'Retain the native initial capacity without growth');

    for(const call of calls){assert.equal(call.pool,pool,'Original7944 must pass the actual World-created pool');assert(call.result&&initialNode(call.result),'Native2243 must return a properly aligned node within the actual initial pool area');assert(call.returnEvent>call.event);assert.equal(call.returnedEip,6261307);}
    const controller=enrollment.state.enrollment.remote.remoteController,sentinel=controller+4;
    assert(readable(f,controller,576));const head=f.get(sentinel),tail=f.get(sentinel+4);
    assert(head&&tail,'Native controller history list must retain nonnull links');

    while(node!==sentinel){
      assert(nodes.length<world.header.totalCapacity,'Bounded native list traversal must reach its actual sentinel');

const fs = require('node:fs');

function create({directory}) {

  let offset = 0, binding = null, closed = false, earlyReportAttempt = null;
  const rows = [], spans = [];

    fs.writeSync(rawFd,bytes); offset += length; spans.push(span); return span;
  }

function initialize(ctx){

  return{starts,sockets,routes,closes};

  state.pendingClient={wholeNativeReturned:true,entry:17624,guest:'0x58f410',...result,before,after,native,newPlatformReturns:newApis,nativeCalleeEspAdvance:4,callerReclaimsBytes:0,pendingClientStateProducedByOriginal:true,sessionReady:false,onlineSessionClaimed:false,gameCarOrControllerCreated:false};writeJson('native-pending-client-proof.json',state.pendingClient);return state.pendingClient;
 });

  function observe({f,marker,cpu:cpuAddress,event,activeStage}){

    const sp=f.get(cpuAddress+16),row={event,activeStage,function:marker.id,phase:marker.phase,cpuHex:f.bytes(cpuAddress,344).toString('hex'),

  }
  function run({q,stage,writeJson}){
    assert(!ran);ran=true;active=true;const{f,A}=q;
    const schedule=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(directory,'timing-caller-inputs.json'))),proof={kind:'ORDINARY-NATIVE-REALTIME-TIMING-CALLER-SUBSET',complete:false,
      inputs:schedule,frequencyInitialization:null,steps:[],failure:null,nativeClockDtRateFlagHistoryOrReportOutputsAuthored:false,
      scope:'At most12 declared60Hz host work intervals before unchanged114. Complete native21992 pacing and21996 remain authoritative; this is not a whole6520/World frame.'};
    // Preserve the actual CPU/FPU. Push only ordinary caller return/argument words.
    function call(id,args,returnAddress,label){

        assert.equal(f.exports.sp.value,shadow);assert.equal(f.get(A.cpu+32),returnAddress);assert.equal(f.get(A.cpu+16),entryEsp+4,'PlainRET must be observed before caller cleanup');
        row.nativeCalleeEspAdvance=4;f.put(A.cpu+16,f.get(A.cpu+16)+4*args.length);row.callerCleanupBytes=4*args.length;row.afterCallerCleanup=cpu(f,A);assert.equal(f.get(A.cpu+16),outerEsp);
      }catch(error){row.failure={name:error.name,message:error.message,stack:error.stack};row.atFailure=cpu(f,A);throw error;}
      finally{proof.lastCall=row;delete proof.activeCall;}
      return row;
    }
    let okay=true;
    try{
      assert.equal(schedule.steps.length,12);assert.equal(schedule.frequencyHz,1000000);assert.equal(f.view.getUint16(A.cpu+140,true),0x37f,'Retain actual default native FPU control');
      proof.before=state(f,A,'before-timing-prerequisite');

        proof.frequencyInitialization={before:state(f,A,'before-whole16627')};
        proof.frequencyInitialization.call=call(16627,[],0x12345678,'whole16627');
        proof.frequencyInitialization.after=state(f,A,'after-whole16627');return proof.frequencyInitialization;
      });
      for(const input of schedule.steps){
        if(!okay)break;const row={input,complete:false};proof.steps.push(row);
        okay=stage('native-realtime-timing-only-input-step'+input.frameId,()=>{
          row.before=state(f,A,'step'+input.frameId+'-before');
          row.previousSampleCall=call(16780,[],0x5811d2,'step'+input.frameId+'-previous16780');row.previousSample=row.previousSampleCall.nativeReturn.eax;
          const hostBefore=getClockMicroseconds(),scheduled=BigInt(input.hostMicroseconds),effective=scheduled>hostBefore?scheduled:hostBefore;
          setClockMicroseconds(effective);assert.equal(getClockMicroseconds(),effective);
          row.platformInput={scheduledMicroseconds:String(scheduled),beforeMicroseconds:String(hostBefore),effectiveMicroseconds:String(effective),monotonicAdvanceMicroseconds:String(effective-hostBefore),nativePacingAdvanceRetained:hostBefore>scheduled};
          row.currentSampleCall=call(16780,[],0x5812da,'step'+input.frameId+'-current16780');row.currentSample=row.currentSampleCall.nativeReturn.eax;
          row.elapsedCall=call(16937,[row.previousSample,row.currentSample],0x5812e1,'step'+input.frameId+'-elapsed16937');
          const top=f.get(A.cpu+136)&7,st0Address=A.cpu+72+8*top,st0Bytes=Buffer.from(f.bytes(st0Address,8)),elapsed=f.view.getFloat64(st0Address,true),control=f.view.getUint16(A.cpu+140,true),constantBits=f.get(7881564),constant=f.view.getFloat32(7881564,true);
          row.elapsed={top,st0Address,rawHex:st0Bytes.toString('hex'),valueMilliseconds:elapsed,controlWord:control,constantAddress:7881564,constantBits,constant};
          assert.equal(control,0x37f);assert.equal(constantBits,981668463);assert(Number.isFinite(elapsed));
          const product=elapsed*constant,value=Math.fround(product),argument=Buffer.alloc(4);argument.writeFloatLE(value);assert(Number.isFinite(product)&&Number.isFinite(value));

          f.put(A.cpu+136,(top+1)&7);assert.deepEqual(f.bytes(st0Address,8),st0Bytes);
          row.callerConversion={productF64:product,argumentF32:value,argumentBits:argument.readUInt32LE(),topBefore:top,topAfter:(top+1)&7,physicalSt0Unchanged:true,
            modeledNormalCallerOnly:true,nativeFpuControlAssigned:false};
          row.frameTimeCall=call(21992,[argument.readUInt32LE()],0x5812f2,'step'+input.frameId+'-whole21992');
          row.afterFrameTime=state(f,A,'step'+input.frameId+'-after21992');

          row.afterRealTimer=state(f,A,'step'+input.frameId+'-after21996');row.complete=true;return row;
        });
      }
      proof.complete=okay&&proof.steps.length===12&&proof.steps.every(row=>row.complete);
    }catch(error){proof.failure={name:error.name,message:error.message,stack:error.stack};proof.complete=false;}
    finally{proof.after=state(f,A,'after-timing-prerequisite');proof.stageFailureRetained=!okay;proof.markerRows=rows.length;proof.spans=spans;writeJson('native-realtime-timing-receipt.json',proof);active=false;}
    return proof;
  }
  function finalize(){assert(!closed);closed=true;fs.closeSync(rawFd);fs.closeSync(journalFd);return{rawSha256:sha(fs.readFileSync(path.join(directory,rawName))),journalSha256:sha(fs.readFileSync(path.join(directory,journalName))),rows:rows.length,spans};}
  return{run,observe,finalize};
}

function permissionAt(address,model){
 if(address<model.base)return{end:model.base,read:true,write:true,source:'committed-fixture'};
 if(address<model.headerEnd)return{end:model.headerEnd,read:true,write:false,source:'PE-headers'};
 if(address>=model.imageEnd)return{end:GUEST_END,read:true,write:true,source:'committed-fixture'};

 return{end:model.imageEnd,read:false,write:false,source:'PE-image-gap'};
}
function checkRange({pointer,count,access,readBitmapByte,availableBytes=GUEST_END,permissions=PE_PERMISSIONS}){

'use strict';
// Source-backed WASI poll_oneoff CLOCK boundary. The native pacer/sleep/GIL path
// owns subscriptions and outcomes; this supplies only the declared host wait.
const fs=require('node:fs'),path=require('node:path'),assert=require('node:assert/strict');
const states=new WeakMap();
function stateFor(f){let state=states.get(f.memory);if(!state){state={calls:[],clockCalls:[],blockedMicroseconds:0n};states.set(f.memory,state);}return state;}
function install(H,{directory,getClockMicroseconds,setClockMicroseconds}){
  assert(!H.boundary[33],'Fresh exact WASI33 boundary only');
  for(const id of[2,7]){
    assert(!H.boundary[id],'Fresh source-backed typed clock shim only');
    H.boundary[id]=f=>{
      const state=stateFor(f),microseconds=id===2?getClockMicroseconds():state.blockedMicroseconds;
      assert(microseconds>=0n&&microseconds<=0xffffffffn,'Typed shim conversion must remain exact unsigned32 microseconds; never wrap');
      const row={api:id===2?'nfsu2_now_ms':'nfsu2_blocked_ms',originalSignature:'()->f64',recorderMicroseconds:String(microseconds),
        originalTypedResultMilliseconds:Number(microseconds)/1000,shim:'exact f64.convert_i32_u /1000.0',guestWritesPerformed:false};
      state.clockCalls.push(row);fs.appendFileSync(path.join(directory,'platform-timing-clock-calls.jsonl'),JSON.stringify(row)+'\n');return Number(microseconds);
    };
  }
  H.boundary[33]=(f,input,output,count,eventsOut)=>{
    const state=stateFor(f),calls=state.calls;
    const row={api:'wasi_snapshot_preview1.poll_oneoff',input,output,count,eventsOut,returned:false};calls.push(row);
    try{
      assert.equal(count,1,'Original24559 single clock subscription only');assert(calls.length<=12,'At most12 native waits in12 timing steps');
      for(const[p,n]of[[input,48],[output,32],[eventsOut,4]])assert(Number.isInteger(p)&&p>=0x10000&&p+n<=f.memory.buffer.byteLength);
      row.subscriptionHex=f.bytes(input,48).toString('hex');row.outputBeforeHex=f.bytes(output,32).toString('hex');row.eventsOutBefore=f.get(eventsOut);
      const tag=f.view.getUint8(input+8),clockId=f.get(input+16),timeout=f.view.getBigUint64(input+24,true),precision=f.view.getBigUint64(input+32,true),flags=f.view.getUint16(input+40,true);
      row.subscription={userdataHex:f.bytes(input,8).toString('hex'),tag,clockId,timeoutNanoseconds:String(timeout),precisionNanoseconds:String(precision),flags};
      assert.equal(tag,0);assert.equal(clockId,0,'Original24560 uses native realtime clockID0 for relative wait');assert.equal(flags,0);assert.equal(precision,0n);
      assert(timeout<=100000000n,'Bounded native pacer wait at most100ms');
      const before=getClockMicroseconds(),advance=(timeout+999n)/1000n;setClockMicroseconds(before+advance);
      assert.equal(getClockMicroseconds(),before+advance);
      state.blockedMicroseconds+=advance;
      // The source game-worker host writes these event fields only; retain padding.
      f.put(output,f.get(input));f.put(output+4,f.get(input+4));f.view.setUint16(output+8,0,true);f.view.setUint8(output+10,tag);f.put(eventsOut,1);
      assert.equal(f.bytes(input,48).toString('hex'),row.subscriptionHex);
      row.hostWait={beforeMicroseconds:String(before),advanceMicroseconds:String(advance),afterMicroseconds:String(getClockMicroseconds()),accumulatedBlockedMicroseconds:String(state.blockedMicroseconds),
        timerQuantizationNanoseconds:String(advance*1000n-timeout),policy:'Integer-microsecond fixture platform timer rounds each exact native requested duration upward by less than1us; no native clock/tick output is written.'};
      row.outputAfterHex=f.bytes(output,32).toString('hex');row.eventsOutAfter=f.get(eventsOut);row.actualResult=0;row.returned=true;return 0;
    }catch(error){row.failure={name:error.name,message:error.message,stack:error.stack};throw error;}
    finally{fs.appendFileSync(path.join(directory,'platform-timing-poll-calls.jsonl'),JSON.stringify(row)+'\n');}
  };
}
function receipt(f){const state=stateFor(f);return{calls:state.calls,clockCalls:state.clockCalls,accumulatedBlockedMicroseconds:String(state.blockedMicroseconds),scope:'Exact typed now_ms and actual accumulated fixture waits in blocked_ms; only native single relative realtime CLOCK subscription: stride48, event32, copied userdata/error0/typeCLOCK, one event and return0.
